3b5(1): skus//sa bon lnga yi ngo bo nyid//rnam bzhir phye zhing phye ba yi//lce rtsa lce rtser gsungs pa ni//khadog dkar po mnyam pa'i 'od//zhing dang chu'i sbyin sreg la//hUM gi rnam pa rkan la sbyar//

21b1(1): brtul zhugs rim pa 'di nyid ni//nan tan gyis ni gtor ma sbyin//de yis las rnams thams cad la'o//ye shes de nyid 'bras bu sogs//de ltar bden pa'i sngags kun
21b2(1): gyis//'grub pa 'di la the tshom med//bza' sogs ston mo'i rnal 'byor pas//zla ba tha ma'i nyi ma bzung //rang 'dod chos ni rab brtags nas//thams cad sngags ni

21b4(1): bzhin//skyon med sngags kyis sbyin byas nas//de yi dus rnams thams cad du//dpal dang phun tshogs mdun nam nas//rnal 'byor chos sogs la dga' bas//gnyis med

21b7(1): dang kun+da zla ba'i 'od//a li'i sa bon mchog gtso bo//dbugs mi g.yo bas bsgom par bya//skad cig rnam par sbyor ba yis//ye shes mkha' 'gro bdag nyid ni//zhal 
22a1(1): @#//gsum phyag ni drug pa nyid//kha dog sngon po mnyam pa'i 'od//sems dpa'i skyil mo krung gis bzhugs//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor 
22a2(1): gyis brgyan par//steng du rdo rje gcod pan dang //sangs rgyas lnga yi dbu la brgyan//rgod dang khro dang sgeg pa dang //spyan gsum gyis ni rnam par brgyan//

22a5(1): pa dang //gnyis pa thod pa khrag gis bkang //ral gri'i phyag ni steng du'o//rdo rje 'od zer dri ma med//sangs rgyas sprin ltar rab 'phro ba//sa yi dbus su rnam rol 
22a6(1): 'bar//dngos pos dngos po bsgom par bya//'ug pa lce spyang seng ge dang //stag mo phyogs su rnam par bsgom//bya rog lce sbyang bya rgod dang //ci la ka ni 
22a7(1): mtshams su dgod//'jig rten sgra sgrogs mche ba brtsigs//ha hUM rnam par 'jigs par byed//'di ltar mkha' 'gro rgyal dbang ma//bdag nyid sems kyis

22b2(1): 'od dang grol ba'i go 'phang gnas//gnyis med gtor ma'i lha nyid yin//sbyor ba'i sgo nas bsgrub pa pos//'di yi mchod pa'i cho ga bshad//bza' dang ston mo'i 
22b3(1): de nyid dang //gsang ba'i mchod pas rab tu mchod//snying kha'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//sems kyis hUM gi sa bon las//de yi gzugs la sogs bskyed 
22b4(1): pa//rdo rje sems dpa'i bdag nyid dgod//bza' ba la sogs khyad par ni//dag pa'i dngos por bsgom nas ni//bsgrub po shin tu mnyam gzhag pas//yud 
22b5(1): tsam gyis ni sbyor ba yis//oM ha ho hri swA hA/oM gis 'bar bar bsams pa dang //ha yi rnam pas (sa) sor rtogs//sa bon ho yis snang bar bya//hri yi rnam 
22b6(1): pas dri ma sbyong //lus kyi lha nyid tshim bya'i phyir//a yis rnam pas rtogs par bya//sa bon lnga yis lha nyid ni//sangs rgyas lnga nyid mngon par thim//oM dzi g+hi tU~M 
22b7(1): a swA hA//bza' ba la sogs khyadapar nyid//sngags 'di yis ni gzung bar bya//oM gi rnam pa'i lus dang ni//dzi gyi rnam pas gzung ba dang //g+h+r-i gyi rnam pa'i 
23a1(1): @#//lto ba dang //tra~M gyi rnam pas dri med lus//hU~M gyi rnam pas spyad par bya//sems kyi snying khar yi ge a//de nyid rtag tu mchod byas na//
23a2(1): rnal 'byor pa yis sdig sbyang bya//dri dang me tog mar me dang //lha shos dang ni gtor ma spos//glu yi dus su dbyangs snyan dang //pi wang gling bu la sogs sbyar//rtag tu 
23a3(1): de nyid kyis mchod cing //rdo rje sems dpa' bdag ces brjod//'gro dang nyal dang 'dug pa dang //'chag dang thams cad dus nyid du//rtag tu sems shing sems dpa' yis 
23a4(1): rdo rje sems dpa' bsam par bya//oM a mu hUM swA hA/ye shes gzugs brnyan sbyor ba yis//sa bon 'di ni bzlas byas na//sdig pa thams cad thag srings shing //

23a6(1): bar 'gyur//oM ka ra ka ra ma hA pra dz+nyA ma hA tig nan pan tsa oM bud+d+he ha oM kro da hUM hUM hUM/a a a/mu mu mu swA hA//sngags chen po yi sa bon 'di//sangs rgyas sems ni mdor 
23a7(1): bsdus pa'o//skyon sbyor ba yi sgrub pa pos//thams cad ldan pa'i sa bon 'di//mtshan ma yi ni mtha' nyid dang //dang por ro hi ta dus la//ha phyis snod du chu dang ni//
23b1(1): ma ga ta ni srang gnyis nyid//kun nas zla ba'i dkyil 'khor bsam//bdud rtsi'i dus su yang dag ldan//bdud rtsi'i bsam pa'i thabs kyis ni//shin tu mnyam gzhag 
23b2(1): rnal 'byor pas//lan bdun mdon par sngags byas pas//de yi chu ni btung byas na//sdig pa thams cad 'jig pa dang //ye shes bdud rtsi ye shes sku//yid bzhung+sa 
23b3(1): shes rab rno ba dang //rlung dang gzi brjid dpal 'phel zhing //kun nas skal ldan ngag dang ni//thos dang shes rab nor dang ni//rlung khris dug dang pan kan dang //lus  
23b4(1): dang blo sogs kyis gzer dang //mig dang yan lag ser ba dang //chu 'di yis ni nad kun 'phrog/oM ra ga ta a pa ra rta ni/sar+b+ba sa pa ha ra na tu/maM hUM bri ki ta kro te hUM phaT 
23b5(1): swA hA//'di yi sngags ni sngags chen po'o//bsrung ba kun gyi bla na med//sngon gyi dus su bzhag byas na//rnal 'byor lus ni bsrung ba'o//lan bdun mngon par sngags 
23b6(1): byas la//gnas lnga ru ni lan gsum mo//sangs rgyas lnga ni mngon bsams nas//sbyi bo'i steng du bkur byas na//rgyal po seng ge'i sgra sgrogs dang //rkun mo dang ni me 
23b7(1): dang chu//rlung dang 'khris pa la sogs nad//'jigs byed 'jigs pa 'joms par byed//oM bi mo ha ni saM mo ha ni/sar+b+ba pri yi ti hUM hUM hUM swA hA//'di yi sngagasani  
24a1(1): @#//sngags chen po//'gro kun rmongs par byed par gsungs//mi byams byams pa skyed byed cing //kun nas 'dza' bar 'gyur ba'o//lag pa g.yon pa yi 
24a2(1): ni chu//snga dro'i dus su kha bkrus na//rnal 'byor pa ni 'gru ba 'dod pas//dgos pa thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//de nas sbyor ba mchog tu bshad//snying po las ni snying po mchog|
24a3(1): rdo rje la sogs bsgom par bya//gnyis su med dang mnyam par 'dres//lag gnyis 'di yis tha dad bsgom//dug med pa yang dug gis bsnun//shin tu mi bzad rmongs pa yis|
24a4(1): rmongs pa rnal 'byor bdag nyid 'jig/'bad pas chos kyi thabs kyis ni//dag pa'i lus ni rnam dag byed//dag pa'i sgron ma ltas pa yis//nyon mongs la sogs mun pa 'joms|
24a5(1): namakha' rdo rje rdo rje la//sems dang sems ni rnam par thim//gcig sogs dngos po sgom pa ni//'di ni rnal 'byor mtshan nyid do//me yi lhag ma sogs lhag ma//dbus dang  
24a6(1): dbus su rnam par 'cher//de yi 'od kyis namakha'i khams//shin tu mnyam par gzhag pas blta//sa bon 'di yi myu gu las//a li ka li'i rnam pa 'byung //sa bon sa bon 
24a7(1): rab 'phro bas//nam mkha'i khams su yang dag gnas//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas pa'i//ro langs ma yi gzugs su bsam//pad+ma'i gdan la rnam par bzhugs|
24b1(1): gdugs dkar po yi mngon par g.yogs//phyag gnyis sems dpa'i skyil mo krung //kha dog dmar po'i 'od mnyam pa//lcags kyu zhags pas sdigs 'dzu ba dang //spyan gsum gyis ni

24b3(1): kyu'i sor mo'i yi ge kU//bzlas shing 'di yis drang bar bya//gzugs garnyan kyi ni yid bskyod cing //rang dbugs kyis ni dgang bar bya//oM ra ga ta b+ha ti hUM shUM swA hA//lcags
24b4(1): kyu'i sngags dang phyag rgya yis//rnal 'byor pa yis 'dod bzhin 'grub//rmongs chen shin tu rmongs pa ni//mnyam par gzhag pas rab tu bsgrub//gdan bzhi pa yi rgyud 
24b5(1): kyis ni//sbyor ba'i gdan gyi gsum pa las//grub pa'i snying po bsdus pa ni//mdor bsdus nas ni btus pa'o//de nas ye shes lus las ni//shin tu mnyam gzhag  
24b6(1): rnal 'byor pas//bza' dang bza' ba'i bsams pa yis//spyang dang rtogs pa'i spyad par bya//dngos po smad pa ma yin zhing //gang yang brtag par mi bya'o//mtho dang 
24b7(1): dma' ba'i rigs la sogs//mnyam nyid lha ni lnga pa'o//gang zhig skye ba 'pho sogs la//sems kyis gnod par mi bya'o//gal te gnod par byed sems na//rnal 'byor 
25a1(1): @#//sdom pa ring bar 'gyur//gang zhig che dang phra ba yi//gzhan rjes rang gi byed pa yi//rku ba'i sems ni mi bya'o//rnal 'byor sdom pa 
25a2(1): ring ba nyid//rdzun dang tshig rtsub phra ma dang //khyal pa'i ngag ni ring du yang //gzhan gyi sems la gnod byas pas//rnal 'byor sdom pa ring ba nyid//don 
25a3(1): la ling+ga rab sbyor bas//sbyor ba'i chos kyi snying po nyid//rmongs pas log bkris ngag sogs kyis//de yi rnal 'byor smon pa nyid//legs par byas pa'i

25b2(1): po med byed dang //sems can don ni a lir bsgyur//phyag rgya bzhi dang ldan pa'i rgya//gsang ba gsang ba bcings pa yis//de yis dbus su bdud rtsi ni//skye shing myu

27a1(1): @#//bza' dang ston mo nye bar mdzes//de yi gzugs brnyan 'dun du ni//mkhas pa yi(sa) ni bsgrub par bya//me tog spos dang dril sogs//gnyis
